## Abstract & Executive Summary

Rounds 1–9 (`2026-10-15` to `2026-10-17`, 21 studies across permuted 1D cellular automata, $6\times 6$ and $9\times 9$ `Sudoku-Extreme`, $6\times 6$ `ShipHunt`, $4\times 4$ and $6\times 6$ `Micro-Stratego`, and `Qwen3-1.7B`/`8B` RL) demonstrated that readout-salience keys (`K3`/`K3c`), weight-tied 3-step normal-equation attention (`looped_attn_T3`), 4-step precision-aware Jacobi clearing (`jacobi_lin_T4_prec`), check-gated leaf self-reads (`m_ql`), and structural Local Inconsistency Resolution (`LIR-Masked`) belief-policy separation each passed pre-registered decision bars within their target domains. Yet Rounds 4–9 relied on four domain-specific search scaffolds—a fixed $L=4$-wave beam schedule, a hardcoded stall entropy threshold $H \ge 0.25$ nats with candidate floor $q > 0.05$, a fixed $n=4$-ply rollout window, and a greedy-locking chain-time step floor $m_{\text{qs}} = \min_t q_{\text{step}}(t) \ge 0.50$ (`AUROC = 0.35` on `Sudoku-Extreme`)—and revealed five sharp boundary failures: (i) symmetric sibling cancellation in first-order marginals on equivalence problems, (ii) $80\%$-shared-prefix multi-counting vs. $20\%$-orthogonal leaf crushing in tree search, (iii) piece-count exclusion vetoes ($21.4\%$ veto rate in `R5-E2`) when pooling mutually exclusive case-splits conjunctively, (iv) strong-root degradation at literal $M_0 = 34$ (`+0.00062` step rise in `R9-E1` Seed 102 Mode B), and (v) unshrunk trust-weighted value pooling losing to uniform pooling across three consecutive rounds (`R6-E3`, `R7-E3`, `R8-E3`) due to shared depth-horizon bias and Kish effective sample size ($\mathrm{ESS}$) collapse.

In Rounds 10–12 (`2026-10-18`), a **3-Round Multi-Harness Society of Thinkers** spanning four distinct frontier model families—**`Gemini 4 Argon`**, **`GLM-5.3`**, **`GPT-6-Astra`**, and **`Claude Opus 5.5 [1m]`** (`[R1S:6-13, R2S:6-11, R3S:6-11]`)—replaced all hand-tuned search scaffolds with the **Bitter-Lesson General Learned-Chain Any-Order Model (`GLC-AOM`)** (`[SC:8-30, WM:1-1375, KVS:1-1619]`), unified evaluation across an **8-family, 12-to-15-environment Multi-Game Suite** with a **Bayesian Bradley–Terry–Davidson (`BTD`) Elo & antisymmetric `mElo` League** and a **Schmid-Leiman 3-Factor Bifactor `Mini-ECI` Capability Index** across compute budgets $B \in \{0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256\}$ (`[PR2:18-122, ELO:1-312, ECI:1-428]`), caught and reclassified the Stage-1/Stage-2 CPU surrogate runners (`B-SURROGATE` `[R2A:18-70, R2S:60-73]`), and executed two consecutive, fully pre-registered, zero-oracle-leakage end-to-end neural campaigns on **`NVIDIA L4` GPUs**: **`R11-GPU-NEURAL`** ($B \le 128$, `2,128.57s` Python runtime, **`$0.66`** GCE GPU spend `[R11:15-44, GPULOG:2177-2189]`) and **`R12-GPU-NEURAL-V2`** ($B \le 256$, `5,190.30s` Python runtime, **`$1.89`** GCE GPU spend; combined `R11+R12` GPU spend **`$2.55`** `[R12:11-34, GPULOG:2190-2205]`). Following Rounds 13–17 and the 8-Monograph Literature Synthesis (`2026-10-18` to `2026-10-19`), **Society Round 6** (`[R6A, R6S]`, adding **`Claude Fable 5.1`**) conducted a line-by-line forensic code audit of Wave-1 (`R18`–`R21`), caught `73` / `75` static AST `v2` (`S1`–`S10`) violations across the 4 Wave-1 benchmark runners, derived runtime counterfactual invariance gates (`S11`–`S14`), and executed **Wave-2 (`R22A`, `R22B`, `R23`)** on `NVIDIA L4` GPUs (`1,883.41s + 449.54s + 162.63s = 2,495.58s`, `$0.54 + $0.21 + $0.14 = $0.89`, **`0` AST `v2` violations**), lifting 4-external-model methodological rigor from **`1.27` to `7.85 / 10` (`+6.58` lift)**, followed by **Wave-3 (`R24A`, `R24B`, & `R25`, `[R24A, JR24A, R24B, JR24B, R25, JR25]`)**: executable Stage-3 runtime counterfactual auditing (`S11`–`S14`, `12.85s` / `12.854s`, **`11/11` combined canaries caught**), `GLC-v3` `CLRS-30` + `OpenSpiel` unification on `NVIDIA L4` GPU (`196.66s`, **#1 `0.7904 ± 0.0050` Micro-F1** at $N=64$ `4x` OOD and `91.4%` `Deep_CFR` `NashConv` gap closed at **`1.5798 ± 0.0589`**), and `R25` `GLC_AOM_v3` In-Network Syndrome Attention + `Kyle-AMM` Collinearity-Damped Price Clearing (`846.88s` on `NVIDIA L4` GPU, lifting `k=0` pure-neural reasoning solve rate from **`31.11%` to `55.83%` (#1 at all $k \in \{0,1,2,4,8\}$)** and slashing decentralized `Kyle-AMM` posterior KL by **`87.9%`–`96.8%`** so **`M5_v3` beats centralized `M1_Global_Backprop_Oracle` on Accuracy, KL, and Brier across all 3 real environments**).

### Five Core Findings of Rounds 10–12:

1. **Multi-Model Society Convergence on Six Mathematical Replacements for Hand-Picked Scaffolds (`Round 1`, `[R1S:26-126]`):** Independently scoring the 6 Charter Dimensions (`D1`–`D6`), all four model families concluded that removing the Rounds 4–9 scaffolds without principled replacements drops risk-adjusted viability from `7.52/10` to `4.92/10`, while six mathematical mechanisms lift projected capability to `7.95/10` (`+3.03` net lift `[R1S:33-44]`):
   - **(C1) Two-Stage Disjunctive-then-Conjunctive Tree Clearing (`[R1S:66-77, KVS:595-862]`):** Stage 1 applies a bottom-up counterfactual Bayes mixture with unexplored complement mass $U_{a,v} = 1 - \sum_{j \in E_{a,v}} q_{C_a}(z_j)$ at case-splits (eliminating piece-count vetoes); Stage 2 decomposes root shifts via the invertible tree path-incidence matrix $V = A D$ into parent-relative telescopic edge innovations $\delta_j = \ell(C_j) - \ell(C_{\mathrm{pa}(j)})$ cleared by Gershgorin row-crowding preconditioned `looped_attn_T3` ($D_{\text{crowd}, r} = \lambda(\beta_r) + \sum_{r'} \beta_{r'} \max(0, \langle k^{\text{edge}}_r, k^{\text{edge}}_{r'} \rangle)$, spectral radius $\|T_b\|_\infty < 1$); Stage 3 applies a nested convex precision gate $\alpha_b$ with pure leaf self-consistency $m_{\text{ql}}(C_j)$ (deleting $m_{\text{qs}} \ge 0.50$).
   - **(C2) Marginal-VoI Tree Controller (`SELECT_NODE(j)`) + Action-Exhaustion Mask `Used(j)` (`[R1S:78-86, KVS:260-385]`):** Differentiating the three-regime weight law $W_{\text{eff}}(N, \rho, \beta) = \frac{N\beta}{\lambda + (1 + (N-1)\rho)\beta}$ yields a closed-form Marginal Value of Information ($\mathrm{MVoI}$) tree priority with zero wave, depth, or stall thresholds.
   - **(C3) Mixture-of-Power-Spherical (`MoPS`) Fractal Prospectus Head (`[R1S:87-95, KVS:388-590]`):** Samples directional plans $p_r \in \mathbb{S}^{D_{\text{amp}}-1}$ projected orthogonal to already-cleared keys via the ridge-damped Gram projector $P_\perp(K_{\text{done}}) = I - K_{\text{done}}^\top (K_{\text{done}} B_{\text{done}} K_{\text{done}}^\top + \lambda_{\text{plan}} I)^{-1} K_{\text{done}} B_{\text{done}}$ and transported along continuing chains.
   - **(C4) Partitioned Even-Parity Bilinear Lemma Space ($Z_{\text{aux}} = Z^W_{\text{aux}} \oplus Z^\Pi_{\text{aux}}$) & Factored Variational Family $\mu = \mu_W \otimes \mu_A$ (`[R1S:96-105, WM:110-370]`):** Even co-fluctuation emission $(U^\top \Delta \ell_V) \odot (V^\top \Delta \ell_V) - \sum_v (u_v^\top \Delta \ell_{V_v})(v_v^\top \Delta \ell_{V_v})$ prevents opposite-sign sibling cancellation (`Member 2 Theorem 1`), while restricting $\mu = \mu_W \otimes \mu_A$ eliminates the inference-time Control-as-Inference optimism bias $\frac{\partial \log \mu^*(z)}{\partial \lambda}\big|_0 = \mathbb{E}[U \mid z] - \mathbb{E}[U] \neq 0$ (`Opus 5.5` & `GPT-6-Astra` proof).
   - **(C5) Depth-Debiased Kish-ESS-Shrunk Trust Pooling (`trust_shrunk`) & Surprise-Discounted Episodic Memory ($M_t$) (`[R1S:138-139, WM:375-560]`):** Subtracting calibrated depth bias $\hat{b}_{\text{hor}}(d_r)$ and enforcing $\mathrm{ESS}(w) \ge \kappa_{\text{ess}} K$ prevents variance inflation against `unif`, while $\beta_{M_t} = \gamma_{\text{mem}} \beta_{\text{cons}}^{(t-1)} \exp(-\lambda_{\text{surp}} \mathcal{S}_t(M_t, o_t))$ flushes contradicted memory.
   - **(C6) Bifactor `Mini-ECI`, Mechanistic Redundancy Law & Antisymmetric `mElo` (`[R1S:116-126, ELO:1-312, ECI:1-428]`):** Connects normal-equation crowding to test-time compute scaling via $\Delta c(B) = \alpha \log\!\big(1 + \frac{B}{1 + \rho(B-1)}\big)$.

2. **Round-2 Forensic Audit Catching `B-SURROGATE` Benchmarks and Enforcing Gate `R11-D0` (`[R2A:18-116, R2S:24-97]`):** In Round 2, Member 12 (`Gemini 4 Argon`) and the three external model families (`GLM-5.3`, `GPT-6-Astra`, `Claude Opus 5.5`) audited `benchmarks/run_r10_e1_e2.py`, `benchmarks/run_r10_e3_e4.py`, and `benchmarks/run_stage2_mini_eci.py` line by line. While scoring the underlying PyTorch mathematical operators and statistical estimators in `src/` at **`8.10 / 10` (`A-EXACT` / `A-LIVE-UNTRAINED`)**, the Society scored the Stage-1/2 benchmark runners at **`1.94 / 10` (`Blind-Emp`)** (`[R2S:33-41]`) upon discovering that `GLCAOMWorldModel` trunks had not been trained via SGD and that multi-budget test curves used oracle-interpolated log-odds surrogates (`0.55 * p_true_ms + 0.45 * p_anc_ms` `[R2A:42]`, `(1 - ls)*0.5 + ls*true_q` `[R2A:39]`, `l0_ship + alpha * 0.86 * (logit_exact - l0_ship)` `[R2A:43]`), a seed-conditional branch (`if m0 == 34 and seed == 102 and b_budget == 2` at `run_r10_e1_e2.py:1351` `[R2A:43]`), and circular `mechanistic_redundancy_delta_c` / `btd_probabilities` data generation (`[R2A:67-68]`). The Society formally reclassified Stage-1 (`R10-E1..E4`) and `Stage-2` scaling curves as **`CONTROLLED-SURROGATE-SIMULATION` (`B-SURROGATE`, Operator Ceiling Verification)** and locked [`benchmarks/PREREG-R11-GPU-NEURAL.md`](../../benchmarks/PREREG-R11-GPU-NEURAL.md) with a mandatory **Static AST (`S1`–`S6`) + Runtime `PoisonTensor` (`R1`–`R4`) Zero-Oracle-Leakage Gate (`R11-D0`)** `[PR11:39-58]`.

3. **Round-11 End-to-End `NVIDIA L4` GPU Neural Verification (`R11-GPU-NEURAL`, `A-LIVE-NEURAL`, `$0.66`, `[R11:13-246, R3A:16-143, R3S:25-114]`):** Executed across 3 independent seeds (`101, 102, 103`, $t_{0.975,2} = 4.303$) on an `NVIDIA L4` GPU (`22.03 GB` VRAM, PyTorch `2.14.1+cu130`, CUDA `13.0`), `R11-GPU-NEURAL` passed **4 of 7 pre-registered decision gates in full (`R11-D0`, `R11-D2`, `R11-D3`, `R11-D5`)** and achieved **3 `PARTIAL PASS` verdicts (`R11-D1`, `R11-D4`, `R11-D6`)** where primary directional and structural tests passed while specific magnitude/comparison sub-clauses failed and are reported as honest negative findings (`[R11:17-26, JR11:gate_verdicts]`):
   - **`R11-D0` (`PASS`):** `0` static AST violations across `src/models/glc_aom.py`, `src/search/kv_tree_search.py`, and `benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py`; `3/3` synthetic leak canaries caught (`S6`); `3/3` `PoisonTensor` traps verified per seed (`R1`); exact bitwise invariance (**`global_max_diff = 0.000000000`**) under test-label permutation and zeroing across all 12 environments $\times$ 3 seeds (`R2`); genuine `AdamW` parameter shift $\|\theta_{\text{trained}} - \theta_{\text{init}}\|_2 = \mathbf{17.9362} \, [16.8611, 19.0114]$ with a **`95.6%`** training loss reduction (`15.0813 -> 0.6579`) and `+14.85%` mean $B=0$ test KL reduction vs. uniform (`R3`, with Seed 101 vs.-init disclosure in §5.2); and **`0.000000000`** cached-vs-uncached KV root equivalence (`R4`) `[R11:19, 48-71]`.
   - **`R11-D1` (`PARTIAL PASS` — Positive Neural Compute Scaling vs. Analytic Ceiling Bar):** On blind test batches, `Unified-Generalist::GLC_Tree_Active_PUCT` (`2,035,868` trunk params) scales `Mini-ECI` monotonically (`7/8` non-decreasing transitions) from **`98.34` ($B=0$) to `103.25` ($B=32$) and `105.30` ($B=128$)**—a real neural search gain of **`+6.95` `Mini-ECI` points** ($\kappa = \mathbf{+0.7637}$ per doubling, $R^2_{\text{log}} = \mathbf{0.9241}$, redundancy law $R^2_{\text{red}} = \mathbf{0.9155}$ with $\hat{\rho}_{\text{IRT}} = 0.04$ and physical Gram crowding $\bar{\rho}_{\text{Gram}}(32) = 0.1536$; `GLC-AOM-KV-Tree` achieves $R^2_{\text{red}} = \mathbf{0.9443} \ge 0.92$ with `+5.43` `Mini-ECI` gain and $\kappa = +0.7389$), lifting verified witness discovery `82.8x` from **`0.52%` ($B=0$) to `43.06%` ($B=128$, `+42.54 pp`)** and mean multiplayer Elo from **`927.4` to `1195.6` (`+268.1` Elo)**.     - **`R24B-E2` (`GLC_AOM_v3` Linear-CFR Infoset Regret Memory on Pure-Neural `OpenSpiel`):** Replacing deterministic test-time tree search with a learned counterfactual advantage head $r_\theta(I, a)$ and Linear-CFR cumulative regret table $R^{(b)}(I, a) = \max\!\big(0, \frac{b-1}{b} R^{(b-1)}(I, a) + r_\theta(I, a)\big)$ (`181,250` matched parameters) cuts 4-game mean `NashConv` at $B=64$ from `1.8701 ± 0.0651` (`GLC_AOM_v2`) to **`1.5798 ± 0.0589` (`-0.2903`, paired $t = -11.92, p = 0.0070$, `1.5635 ± 0.0636` at $B=4$)**—closing **`91.4%` of the gap to `Deep_CFR` (`1.5541 ± 0.1745`, statistical tie $t = +0.25, p = 0.8237$, with `3.0x` lower cross-seed SD)**, cutting `leduc_poker` `NashConv` by **`-1.5110` (`6.2167 -> 4.7057` at $B=64$, `4.5200` at $B=4$)**, and beating `Deep_CFR` on `kuhn_poker` (`GLC_AOM_v3_Generalist` **`0.4028 ± 0.0573`** at $B=4$, #1 overall), `phantom_ttt` (`0.3804` / `0.4484` vs. `0.4710`), and `dark_hex` (`0.5918` / `0.6520` vs. `0.7781`).

## 1. The Multi-Harness Society of Thinkers (Rounds 1–3)

### 1.1 Cross-Model Protocol and Fleet Governance

To eliminate single-model blind spots and enforce Bitter-Lesson discipline (`[SC:4-30]`), we convened a **3-Round Society of Thinkers** across four independent model families operating under read-only proxy health verification and zero silent model fallbacks (`CLAUDE_CODE_NO_MODEL_FALLBACK=1`, Fleet Policies 3, 6 & 15 `[R1S:16-23, R2S:14-21, R3S:15-22]`):
1. **`Gemini 4 Argon` (`agy`):** Members 1, 2, 3 (Round-1 mathematical architecture & red-team proofs `[R1S:6-9]`), Member 12 (Round-2 line-by-line forensic code audit `[R2S:6-7]`), Member 14 (Round-3 `R11-GPU-NEURAL` audit & Round-3 scoreboard synthesis `[R3A:1-14, R3S:1-115]`), and Member 15 (morning synthesis & numerical verification).
2. **`GLM-5.3` (`zai-coding-plan/glm-5.3` via `/home/agent/bin/glm`):** Member 4a (`[R1S:10, R2S:8, R3S:8]`), contributing the closed-form Marginal-VoI tree controller derived from $\partial W_{\text{eff}}/\partial N$, VICReg anti-collapse on $Z^W_{\text{aux}}$, Dual-Channel Readout (*"gate weights, never evidence"*), and the Mechanistic Redundancy Law $\Delta c(B) = \alpha \log\!\big(1 + \frac{B}{1 + \rho(B-1)}\big)$ with physical Gram-crowding prediction `P9` (`R3` realized mean `8.22`, `Proj-R12` `8.93`).
3. **`GPT-6-Astra` (`gpt-6-astra` via `codex-any` on `127.0.0.1:7810`):** Member 4b (`[R1S:11, R2S:9, R3S:9]`), contributing the invertible tree path-incidence operator $V = A D$, signed Leave-Subtree-Out (`LSO`) proper-score innovation credit, metric-scaled prospectus plans $(p, \rho)$, antisymmetric `mElo` ($u_i^\top J u_j$), interventional bias audit $D_{\text{bias}}$ under $\mathrm{do}(y_i = y)$, the static-AST + runtime `R11-D0` zero-oracle-leakage firewall specification, and the Round-3 calibration vs. refinement decomposition $\mathbb{E}[D_{\text{KL}}(\mathrm{Ber}(P)\Vert \mathrm{Ber}(Q))] = \mathcal{C} + \mathcal{F}$ (`R3` realized mean `7.72`, `Proj-R12` `8.77`).
4. **`Claude Opus 5.5 [1m]` (`claude-opus-5-5[1m]` via `claude-any` on `127.0.0.1:7800`, `board-opus` standard):** Member 4c (`[R1S:12, R2S:10, R3S:10]`), contributing the 12-scaffold removal ledger, the Control-as-Inference optimism proof ($\frac{\partial \log \mu^*(z)}{\partial \lambda}\big|_0 = \mathbb{E}[U\mid z] - \mathbb{E}[U] \neq 0$) and factored variational family $\mu = \mu_W \otimes \mu_A$, the $Z^W_{\text{aux}} \oplus Z^\Pi_{\text{aux}}$ structural firewall, the second-order Bregman / CGF convex gain certificate $\Gamma_b$ and Gain Capture Ratio $\mathrm{GCR}(B)$, the 3-tier evidentiary classification (`A-EXACT`, `A-LIVE-UNTRAINED`, `B-SURROGATE`), calibrated L4 GPU effect-size priors (`[PRIOR]`: `[+3.5, +11.0]` `Mini-ECI` vs. realized `+6.95`), the `PoisonTensor` sentinel architecture, and the Round-3 `X1`–`X12` nuance ledger (`R3` realized mean `6.33`, `Proj-R12` `7.35`).

## 2. Bitter-Lesson `GLC-AOM` Architecture & Mathematical Proofs

All components of `GLC-AOM` are implemented as reusable PyTorch modules in [`src/models/glc_aom.py`](../../src/models/glc_aom.py) (`1,375` lines) and [`src/search/kv_tree_search.py`](../../src/search/kv_tree_search.py) (`1,619` lines) with zero domain-specific branching or per-game constants (`[PR10:34-46]`).

### 2.1 Universal KV-Cache Tree Machine (`TreePagedKVBank`, `SELECT_NODE(j)`, `Used(j)`, and Marginal VoI)

At search step $b \in \{0, 1, \dots, B-1\}$, the search state is a tree-paged bank of KV-cache checkpoints $\mathcal{C}_b = \{C_0, C_1, \dots, C_b\}$ stored in `TreePagedKVBank` (`[KVS:72-258]`). Each node $C_j$ is created by clamping one variable-value assignment $(v_j, z_j)$ onto its parent $C_{\mathrm{pa}(j)}$ in $O(1)$ memory operations:
1. **Bit-Mask Ancestor Inheritance (`[KVS:240-243]`):** Instead of copying prefix KV tensors, `TreePagedKVBank.append_node` copies the boolean ancestor row $M_{\text{tree}}[b+1, :] = M_{\text{tree}}[j_{b+1}, :] \lor \mathbf{e}_{b+1}^\top$ in $O(1)$ time and appends a single incremental KV page `kv_bank[:, :, :, b+1, :]`. Verified in `R11-D0` (`R4`), incremental KV-paged evaluation matches full sequence re-evaluation to **`0.000000000`** max absolute difference across all 3 seeds (`[R11:65-70]`).
2. **Action-Exhaustion Mask `Used(j)` & Canonical Transposition Merging (`[KVS:92-94, 250-258]`):** Each node $C_j$ tracks a boolean tensor $\mathrm{Used}(j) \in \{0, 1\}^{|V| \times K_{\text{dom}}}$ marking already-expanded child transitions $(v, z)$, plus a canonical sorted assignment-set hash $\mathrm{canon}(O_j) = \mathrm{sort}\{(v_m, z_m)\}_{m \in O_j}$ so permutation-equivalent partial worlds merge immediately (`[R1S:72]`).
3. **Closed-Form Marginal-VoI (`MVoI`) + Resolvable-Inconsistency Tree-PUCT (`[R1S:78-86, KVS:260-385]`):**
   In the correlated-signal normal equations $(K^\top B K + \lambda I) w = K^\top B v$ (`[research-direction-note-v4:57-61]`), $N_j$ rollouts with pairwise source overlap $\rho_j$ and trust precision $\beta_j$ have combined effective weight $W_{\text{eff}}(N_j, \rho_j, \beta_j) = \frac{N_j \beta_j}{\lambda + (1 + (N_j - 1)\rho_j)\beta_j}$. Differentiating $W_{\text{eff}}$ with respect to $N_j$ (`GLM-5.3` derivation `[R1S:81-83]`) gives the exact marginal value of an additional expansion from subtree $C_j$:
   $$\frac{\partial W_{\text{eff}}(N_j, \rho_j, \beta_j)}{\partial N_j} \;=\; \frac{\beta_j\big(\lambda + (1 - \rho_j)\beta_j\big)}{\Big(\lambda + \big(1 + (N_j - 1)\rho_j\big)\beta_j\Big)^2}.$$
   Combining this marginal weight gain with the node's leaf self-consistency $m_{\text{ql}}(C_j)$, depth-debiased trust precision $\beta_{\text{trust}}(C_j)$, and maximum unresolved variable entropy $R_j = \max_{v \notin O_j} I_{\text{goal}}(v \mid C_j)\, H\!\big(q_{C_j}(V_v)\big)$ defines the `ResolvableInconsistencyTreePUCT` priority (`[KVS:285-340]`):
   $$\mathrm{Score}_{\text{node}}(j \mid \mathcal{C}_b) \;=\; \hat{Q}_{\text{tree}}(C_j) \;+\; c_{\text{tree}} \cdot m_{\text{ql}}(C_j) \cdot \beta_{\text{trust}}(C_j) \cdot R_j \cdot \frac{\lambda + (1 - \rho_j)\beta_j}{\Big(\lambda + \big(1 + N_{\text{exp}}(j)\rho_j\big)\beta_j\Big)\sqrt{1 + N_{\text{exp}}(j)}}.$$

> **Proposition 1 (Immunity to Root-Star and Endless-Chain Collapse, `[R1-01, R1-03, KVS:265-345]`):**
> Under `Score_node(j)` with `Used(j)` masking:
> 1. *No Root-Star Collapse ($f_{\text{root}} < 1$):* Whenever root $C_0$ is expanded $N_{\text{exp}}(0)$ times along correlated directions ($\rho_0 > 0$), its marginal factor decays as $O(N_{\text{exp}}(0)^{-3/2})$, while a newly created consistent tip $C_b$ ($m_{\text{ql}}(C_b) \approx 1, N_{\text{exp}}(b) = 0$) with unresolved conditionals ($R_b > 0$) has full denominator $1$, strictly exceeding $\mathrm{Score}_{\text{node}}(0)$ once $N_{\text{exp}}(0)$ passes a finite threshold.
> 2. *No Endless-Chain Collapse ($f_{\text{tip}} < 1$):* Along any continuing chain $C_0 \to C_1 \to \dots \to C_d$, either (a) a clash occurs so $m_{\text{ql}}(C_d) \to 0$, or (b) all variables become clamped/resolved ($O_d \to V$) so $R_d = \max_{v \notin O_d} H(q_{C_d}(V_v)) \to 0$ and $\mathrm{Exp}(d) = 0$, forcing `SELECT_NODE` to fork from an earlier high-entropy ancestor $C_j$ ($0 \le j < d$).
> 3. *Emergent Forced-Single Absorption:* When a variable $v \notin O_j$ is nearly determined ($H(q_{C_j}(V_v)) \to 0$), Gumbel-top-$k$ step selection (`_select_step_var_and_val` `[KVS:490-565]`) absorbs the forced assignment along the current tip before branching at high-entropy stalls, without any hardcoded $H \ge 0.25$ threshold or $r=0$ greedy lead chain.

### 2.2 Active Fractal Prospectus Plans (`MoPSFractalPlanHead`, Gram Orthogonalization & Residual Transport)

To steer multi-step chains toward complementary information subspaces without fixed wave schedules (`[SC:16-18, R1S:87-95, KVS:388-590]`), `MoPSFractalPlanHead` parameterizes a directional distribution over the amplified space $\mathbb{S}^{D_{\text{amp}}-1}$ paired with a target scale $\rho_r \ge 0$:
1. **Mixture of Power Spherical (`MoPS`) Density (`[R1-01, KVS:395-450]`):** With $M_{\text{modes}}$ learned mixture modes $\{\mu_m(C_j), \kappa_m(C_j), \pi_m(C_j)\}_{m=1}^{M_{\text{modes}}}$, each component has closed-form density $f_{\text{PS}}(p; \mu_m, \kappa_m) \propto (1 + \mu_m^\top p)^{\kappa_m}$ on $\mathbb{S}^{D_{\text{amp}}-1}$, avoiding the Overflow/Bessel-function intractability of von Mises–Fisher distributions at $D_{\text{amp}} = 1{,}066$ while supporting exact reparameterized sampling via $(t, v_\perp) \sim \mathrm{Beta}\!\big(\frac{D_{\text{amp}}-1}{2} + \kappa_m, \frac{D_{\text{amp}}-1}{2}\big) \times \mathcal{U}(\mathbb{S}^{D_{\text{amp}}-2})$.
2. **Ridge-Damped Gram Orthogonal Projection (`[KVS:452-488]`):** Given the matrix of already-explored edge-salience keys $K_{\text{done}} \in \mathbb{R}^{b \times D_{\text{amp}}}$ and diagonal trust precisions $B_{\text{done}} = \mathrm{diag}(\beta_1, \dots, \beta_b)$, raw sampled plans $\tilde{p}$ are projected onto the unexplored complement via the Woodbury identity in $O(b^2 D_{\text{amp}} + b^3)$ operations:
   $$P_\perp(K_{\text{done}}) \tilde{p} \;=\; \tilde{p} \;-\; K_{\text{done}}^\top \big(K_{\text{done}} B_{\text{done}} K_{\text{done}}^\top + \lambda_{\text{plan}} I_b\big)^{-1} K_{\text{done}} B_{\text{done}} \tilde{p}, \qquad p_{b+1} \;=\; \frac{P_\perp(K_{\text{done}}) \tilde{p}}{\|P_\perp(K_{\text{done}}) \tilde{p}\|_2 + \epsilon}.$$
3. **Orthogonal Residual Plan Transport (`[KVS:475-488]`):** When `SELECT_NODE` continues the current tip ($j_{b+1} = b$), the active plan is transported by subtracting the realized step edge key $k^{\text{edge}}_b$:
   $$p_{b+1} \;=\; \mathrm{normalize}\!\Big(p_b \;-\; \langle p_b, \, k^{\text{edge}}_b \rangle \, k^{\text{edge}}_b\Big),$$
   whereas when `SELECT_NODE` forks from an ancestor ($j_{b+1} < b$), a fresh orthogonalized plan is sampled from `MoPSFractalPlanHead`.

### 2.3 Partitioned Bidirectional Lemma Space ($Z_{\text{aux}} = Z^W_{\text{aux}} \oplus Z^\Pi_{\text{aux}}$), Even-Parity Bilinear Emission, and Combinatorial Queries $Q(A, B)$

To overcome the first-order marginal equivalence barrier while maintaining zero utility leakage (`[R1S:96-105, WM:110-370]`):

> **Theorem 1 (First-Order Marginal Cancellation vs. Even-Parity Bilinear Lemma Sufficiency, `[R1-02 §1.2, WM:110-225]`):**
> Let $X_A, X_B \in \{0, 1\}$ be unrevealed variables with symmetric prior marginals $P^*(X_A = 1 \mid x_O) = P^*(X_B = 1 \mid x_O) = \frac{1}{2}$ coupled by an unobserved multi-step parity/equivalence chain $C_{AB}$ such that $P^*(X_A = X_B \mid x_O) = 1 - \varepsilon$. Suppose a search tree forks at $C_0$ into two symmetric branches: $C_1$ clamping $X_A = 0$ (which propagates through $C_{AB}$ to yield log-odds shift $\Delta \ell_B^{(1)} = -\Lambda \mathbf{e}$) and $C_2$ clamping $X_A = 1$ (which yields $\Delta \ell_B^{(2)} = +\Lambda \mathbf{e}$).
> 1. *First-Order Cancellation:* Any permutation-symmetric linear pool over first-order variable log-odds $\Delta \ell_V \in \mathbb{R}^{D_V}$ assigns equal weight $w_1 = w_2 = w$ to $C_1$ and $C_2$, yielding cleared marginal shift $w \Delta \ell_V^{(1)} + w \Delta \ell_V^{(2)} = w(-\Lambda + \Lambda) = \mathbf{0}$, losing all information about the coupling $X_A \equiv X_B$.
> 2. *Odd Linear Auxiliary Cancellation:* Any odd linear auxiliary emitter $z_{\text{aux}} = W_{\text{lin}} \Delta \ell_V$ (`A2_LinearAux`) satisfies $z_{\text{aux}}(-\Delta \ell_V) = -z_{\text{aux}}(+\Delta \ell_V)$ and likewise cancels to $\mathbf{0}$.
> 3. *Even-Parity Bilinear Non-Cancellation:* The cross-variable bilinear co-fluctuation emitter implemented in `BilinearAuxLemmaEmitter.forward` (`[WM:191-225]`),
>    $$z_{\text{aux}}(\Delta \ell_V) \;=\; (U \tilde{\Delta \ell}_V) \odot (V \tilde{\Delta \ell}_V) \;-\; \sum_{v=1}^{|V|} (U_{:, v} \tilde{\Delta \ell}_{V_v}) \odot (V_{:, v} \tilde{\Delta \ell}_{V_v}), \qquad \tilde{\Delta \ell}_V = 3 \tanh(\Delta \ell_V / 3),$$
>    is strictly even: $z_{\text{aux}}(-\Delta \ell_V) \equiv z_{\text{aux}}(+\Delta \ell_V)$ (verified to **`0.000000`** max error across all seeds in `R10-E1` and `R11-GPU-NEURAL` `[R11:237]`), and subtracts single-variable self-squares ($v = v'$) so $z_{\text{aux}}$ encodes pure cross-variable co-fluctuations $\Delta \ell_{V_A} \otimes \Delta \ell_{V_B}$. Both branches $C_1$ and $C_2$ write the identical positive code $z_{\text{aux}}^{(1)} = z_{\text{aux}}^{(2)} \neq \mathbf{0}$ into $Z^W_{\text{aux}}$.
> 4. *Bidirectional Top-Down Forcing:* Minimizing the random-permutation any-order loss $\mathcal{L}_{\text{any-order}}(h_{\text{cons}}; x^*) = -\mathbb{E}_\pi \sum_t \log q_\theta(x^*_{\pi(t)} \mid x_O, x^*_{\pi(<t)}, h_{\text{cons}})$ (`[SC:23, WM:1150-1210]`) over permutations where $A$ precedes $B$ ($\pi^{-1}(A) < \pi^{-1}(B)$) and where $B$ precedes $A$ ($\pi^{-1}(B) < \pi^{-1}(A)$) forces the top-down decoder to recover $P^*(X_B \mid X_A, Z^W_{\text{aux}})$ and $P^*(X_A \mid X_B, Z^W_{\text{aux}})$ from the same cleared vector $h_{\text{cons}}$.

**Structural Partition & Control-as-Inference Optimism Proof (`[R1-06 §4.2, WM:97-104, 167-173]`):**
In `research-direction-note-v4.md` §2.1, multiplying a joint distribution $\mu(z, a, y)$ by $f_U(y) \propto \exp(\lambda U(y))$ creates an inference-time optimism leak on uncontrolled latent variables $z$:
$$\frac{\partial \log \mu^*(z)}{\partial \lambda}\Big|_{\lambda=0} \;=\; \mathbb{E}_{\mu_0}[U(Y) \mid Z = z] \;-\; \mathbb{E}_{\mu_0}[U(Y)] \;\neq\; 0.$$
`GLC-AOM` eliminates this both at training time and at inference time by restricting the variational family to $\mu(z, y, a \mid o) = \mu_W(z, y \mid o, \mathrm{do}(a)) \cdot \pi_U(a \mid o)$ and partitioning $Z_{\text{aux}} = Z^W_{\text{aux}} \oplus Z^\Pi_{\text{aux}}$ (`d_aux_world = 128`, `d_aux_plan = 128` `[WM:96-104]`):
- `BilinearAuxLemmaEmitter.mask_to_world_lemma_channel` (`[WM:167-173]`) zeros out `Z^Pi_aux` (`h_aux[..., d_aux_world:] = 0`) before any variable or query belief readout, guaranteeing $\|\partial \ell_V / \partial Z^\Pi_{\text{aux}}\|_\infty \equiv \mathbf{0.000000}$ by autograd construction (`[R10A:15, R11:204]`).
- `CombinatorialQueryHead` (`[WM:275-370]`) embeds relational queries $Q(A, B, r)$ (`EQUAL`, `NOT_EQUAL`, `PARITY_XOR`, `GREATER`, `LESS`), seeds the initial prospectus $p_0 = k_{Q(A, B)}$ to steer tree search toward $\{A, B\}$, and outputs both the direct relational probability $\hat{P}_{\text{direct}}(Q(A, B) \mid h_{\text{cons}})$ and the 2-step autoregressive readout $\hat{P}_{\text{AR}}(X_A = X_B \mid h_{\text{cons}})$ with discrepancy $\delta_{\text{rel}}(A, B) = |\hat{P}_{\text{direct}} - \hat{P}_{\text{AR}}|$.

### 2.4 Present-Time Episodic Memory (`EpisodicMemoryTransport`) and Depth-Debiased `trust_shrunk` Pooling

In sequential imperfect-information games (`Micro-Stratego`, `Galanis-Market`, `Proposal-Poker`), re-simulating history from $t=0$ violates constant-depth test-time scaling, while naively clamping stale beliefs $h_{\text{cons}}^{(t-1)}$ causes dogmatic error accumulation when an opponent reveals an unexpected piece (`[R1-02 §3, R1-03 §2 Fix 1.4]`). `EpisodicMemoryTransport` (`[WM:375-452]`) transports $h_{\text{cons}}^{(t-1)}$ into $K_{\text{mem}} = 8$ root memory tokens $M_t \in \mathbb{R}^{K_{\text{mem}} \times d_{\text{model}}}$ weighted by a **surprise-discounted precision gate**:
$$\mathcal{S}_t(M_t, o_t) \;=\; \Big[-\log q_{\text{real}}(o_t \mid h_t, M_t) \;+\; \log q_{\text{real}}(o_t \mid h_t, \varnothing)\Big]_+, \qquad \beta_{M_t} \;=\; \gamma_{\text{mem}} \beta_{\text{cons}}^{(t-1)} \exp\!\big(-\lambda_{\text{surp}} \mathcal{S}_t(M_t, o_t)\big).$$
When current observations $o_t$ contradict $M_t$ ($\mathcal{S}_t > 0$), $\beta_{M_t}$ decays exponentially, bounding the multi-turn epistemic error contraction ratio $\hat{\kappa} = \mathrm{KL}_{t+1} / \mathrm{KL}_t < 1$.

For rollout value pooling across heterogeneous tree depths $d_r \in \{1, \dots, D_{\max}\}$, `DepthDebiasedTrustHead` (`[WM:455-560]`) resolves the 3-round `R6-E3..R8-E3` failure where raw $\theta_{\text{trust}}$ lost to uniform pooling (`0.01394` vs. `0.01366` `[experiments-rounds-4-9-paper-v2:39]`):
1. It fits a depth-horizon bias table $\hat{b}_{\text{hor}}(d)$ on calibration rollouts and subtracts $\hat{b}_{\text{hor}}(d_r)$ from each rollout value claim $\hat{v}_r^{\text{deb}} = \hat{v}_r - \hat{b}_{\text{hor}}(d_r)$ before estimating excess surprise $\hat{s}_{\text{excess}, r}$.
2. It maps $\beta_r = (\hat{s}_{\text{excess}, r} + \epsilon_0)^{-1}$ to weights $w_r(\gamma_{\text{trust}}) = \frac{\beta_r^{\gamma_{\text{trust}}}}{\sum_{j=1}^K \beta_j^{\gamma_{\text{trust}}}}$ and selects the temperature $\gamma_{\text{trust}} \in [0, 1]$ subject to the **Kish Effective Sample Size constraint** (`[WM:512-558]`):
   $$\mathrm{ESS}(w) \;=\; \frac{\big(\sum_{r=1}^K w_r\big)^2}{\sum_{r=1}^K w_r^2} \;\ge\; \kappa_{\text{ess}} K \qquad (\kappa_{\text{ess}} = 0.50).$$
   By bounding the variance inflation factor $\frac{1}{\mathrm{ESS}(w)} \le \frac{2}{K}$ while downweighting low-trust hallucinated rollouts, `pool_values_trust_shrunk` strictly dominates both stiffness pooling and uniform pooling whenever rollout reliability is heterogeneous.

### 2.5 `TwoStageTreeClearer`: Disjunctive Bayes Mixture, Gershgorin Preconditioned Clearing, and Convex Certificate Gate

`TwoStageTreeClearer` (`[KVS:595-935]`, `1,030` trainable calibration parameters $\le 2,000$ bar `[R11:245]`) clears the KV-cache search tree $\mathcal{C}_b$ in three exact stages:

1. **Stage 1 — Bottom-Up Disjunctive Counterfactual Bayes Mixture (`stage1_disjunctive_bayes_mixture`, `[KVS:625-675]`):**
   At any node $C_a$ where multiple children $E_{a,v} \subseteq \mathrm{Ch}(a, v)$ clamp mutually exclusive values $\{z_j\}_{j \in E_{a,v}}$ of the same variable $v$, their subtree distributions $\mu_{\text{sub}}(Y \mid C_j)$ represent disjoint events and **must be marginalized in probability space** together with the unexplored prior complement mass $U_{a,v} = \big[1 - \sum_{j \in E_{a,v}} q_{C_a}(V_v = z_j)\big]_+$:
   $$\tilde{w}_j \;=\; q_{C_a}(V_v = z_j) \cdot m_{\text{ql}}(C_j), \qquad m_j \;=\; \frac{\tilde{w}_j}{\sum_{j' \in E_{a,v}} \tilde{w}_{j'} + U_{a,v} + \epsilon},$$
   $$\mu_{\text{disj}}(Y \mid C_a, v) \;=\; \sum_{j \in E_{a,v}} m_j \, \mu_{\text{sub}}(Y \mid C_j) \;+\; \Big(1 - \sum_{j \in E_{a,v}} m_j\Big) \mu(Y \mid C_a).$$
   Because $\mu(Y \mid C_a)$ has full support over all remaining piece types, $\mu_{\text{disj}}(Y \mid C_a, v)$ never assigns zero probability to an unchosen piece hypothesis unless all $K_{\text{dom}}$ values have been exhaustively explored ($U_{a,v} = 0$) and refuted, mathematically eliminating the `R5-E2` piece-count exclusion veto.

2. **Stage 2 — Parent-Relative Telescopic Edge Clearing & Gershgorin Spectral Contraction (`stage2_preconditioned_clearing`, `[KVS:677-778]`):**
   Let $A \in \{0, 1\}^{b \times b}$ be the invertible lower-triangular tree path-incidence matrix ($A_{re} = \mathbf{1}\{e \in \mathrm{path}(C_0 \to C_r)\}$). Because root-relative shifts satisfy $V = A D$, `TwoStageTreeClearer` operates directly on the **parent-relative edge innovations** $\delta_j = \ell(C_j) - \ell(C_{\mathrm{pa}(j)})$ with bag-centered edge-salience keys $k^{\text{edge}}_j = \mathrm{normalize}\big(|\delta_j|/\|\delta_j\|_2 - \bar{s}_{\text{bag}}\big)$. Define the non-negative edge Gram matrix $G^+_{r r'} = \max(0, \langle k^{\text{edge}}_r, k^{\text{edge}}_{r'} \rangle)$, precision-dependent ridge $\lambda(\beta_r) = \operatorname{softplus}(a_{\text{ridge}} - b_{\text{ridge}} \beta_r)$, and **Gershgorin row-crowding preconditioner**:
   $$D_{\text{crowd}, r} \;=\; \lambda(\beta_r) \;+\; \sum_{r'=1}^b \beta_{r'} G^+_{r r'}.$$

> **Theorem 2 (Gershgorin Spectral Contraction and Anytime Monotonicity of Stage 2 & Stage 3, `[R1-03 Theorem 1, R1-06 Appendix A.1, KVS:680-862]`):**
> 1. *Strict Spectral Contraction for All $b \in [1, 128+]$:* For the preconditioned Richardson / Jacobi iteration $w^{(t+1)} = (I - \eta D_{\text{crowd}}^{-1}(G^+ B + \Lambda)) w^{(t)} + \eta D_{\text{crowd}}^{-1} \beta$ with step size $\eta \in (0, 1]$, every row $r$ of $M = D_{\text{crowd}}^{-1}(G^+ B + \Lambda)$ has non-negative off-diagonal entries $M_{r r'} = \frac{\beta_{r'} G^+_{r r'}}{D_{\text{crowd}, r}} \ge 0$ and exact row sum:
>    $$\sum_{r'=1}^b M_{r r'} \;=\; \frac{\lambda(\beta_r) + \sum_{r'=1}^b \beta_{r'} G^+_{r r'}}{D_{\text{crowd}, r}} \;\equiv\; 1.$$
>    By the Gershgorin Circle Theorem, every eigenvalue $\mu_i(M)$ lies in the disk $\big|\mu_i - M_{rr}\big| \le 1 - M_{rr}$ with $M_{rr} = \frac{\lambda(\beta_r) + \beta_r}{D_{\text{crowd}, r}} > 0$, so the iteration operator $T_b = I - \eta M$ satisfies $\|T_b\|_\infty \le 1 - \eta \min_r \frac{\lambda(\beta_r)}{\lambda(\beta_r) + \sum_{r'} \beta_{r'} G^+_{r r'}} < 1$ for all $b \ge 1$ (`0.999947 < 1.0` in `R10-E3` and **`0.998281 < 1.0`** in `R11-GPU-NEURAL` `[R11:243]`).
> 2. *80%-Prefix Deduplication & 20%-Orthogonal Leaf Retention:* When $M$ leaves share an 8-step trunk $C_0 \to C_a$ and branch into $M$ orthogonal 2-step leaves $C_{j_1}, \dots, C_{j_M}$, the trunk edges $e \in \mathrm{path}(C_0 \to C_a)$ appear once in $\mathcal{C}_b$ ($R_{\text{prefix}}(M) = \frac{1 + \lambda(1)}{1 + \lambda(1)} \approx 1$), while the orthogonal post-fork edges satisfy $\langle k^{\text{edge}}_{j_m}, k^{\text{edge}}_{j_{m'}}\rangle = 0$ so $D_{\text{crowd}, j_m} = \lambda(\beta) + \beta$ is independent of $M$ ($R_{\text{leaf}}(M) \equiv 1.0000$).
> 3. *Second-Order Bregman / CGF Convex Gate (Stage 3):* Let $\ell^{(b)}$ be the current belief logits ($\mu_b = \mathrm{softmax}(\ell^{(b)})$) and $\tilde{\ell}^{(b+1)} = \ell^{(0)} + \Delta \ell_{\text{cleared}}^{(b+1)}$ be the candidate cleared logits with residual $r_b = \tilde{\ell}^{(b+1)} - \ell^{(b)}$. For the convex interpolation $\ell(\alpha) = (1 - \alpha)\ell^{(b)} + \alpha \tilde{\ell}^{(b+1)}$ ($\alpha \in [0, 1]$), the exact KL difference to the true posterior $P^*$ is given by the cumulant-generating-function expansion:
>    $$\mathrm{KL}(P^* \Vert \mu(\alpha)) - \mathrm{KL}(P^* \Vert \mu_b) \;=\; -\alpha \underbrace{\big(\mathbb{E}_{P^*}[r_b] - \mathbb{E}_{\mu_b}[r_b]\big)}_{\Gamma_b \text{ (Linear Bregman Gain)}} \;+\; \int_0^\alpha (\alpha - u)\,\mathrm{Var}_{\mu(u)}(r_b)\,du \;\le\; -\alpha \Gamma_b + \frac{\alpha^2}{2} \sup_{u \in [0,1]} \mathrm{Var}_{\mu(u)}(r_b).$$
>    Setting $\alpha^*_b = \mathrm{clip}\!\Big(\frac{[\widehat{\Gamma}_b - \varepsilon_{\text{cal}}]_+}{\sup_u \mathrm{Var}_{\mu(u)}(r_b) + \epsilon_0},\; 0,\; 1\Big)$ guarantees $\mathrm{KL}(P^* \Vert \mu(\alpha^*_b)) \le \mathrm{KL}(P^* \Vert \mu_b)$ whenever $|\widehat{\Gamma}_b - \Gamma_b| \le \varepsilon_{\text{cal}}$, with exact zero-step identity $\ell^{(0)} = \ell_{\text{root}}$ at $b = 0$.

## 3. Multi-Game Benchmark Suite, Bayesian Elo / `mElo` League & Bifactor `Mini-ECI`

### 3.1 The 8 Environment Families (12 Core `R11` Environments / 15 `Stage-2` Configurations)

All environments inherit from `UnifiedGameEnv` in [`src/envs/base.py`](../../src/envs/base.py) and emit `EnvBatch` instances with a standardized 6-token header (`HEADER_LEN = 6`: `[GAME_FAMILY, GAME_ID, GRID_H, GRID_W, INVENTORY_SPEC, UTILITY_SPEC]` `[PR2:19-20]`), allowing a single `GLCAOMWorldModel` checkpoint (`Unified-Generalist`) to process every game without task-specific heads. Table 3 lists the complete benchmark suite (`[PR2:23-34, R11:63]`).

### 3.2 Bayesian Bradley–Terry–Davidson (`BTD`) Elo & Antisymmetric `mElo` (`src/eval/elo_league.py`)

In [`src/eval/elo_league.py`](../../src/eval/elo_league.py) (`[ELO:1-312, PR2:76-89]`), head-to-head match outcomes (win/draw/loss) across seat-mirrored duplicate pairs ($s \in \{-1, 0, +1\}$) are modeled by the regularized Bradley–Terry–Davidson likelihood with per-environment first-mover advantage $\gamma_e$ and draw parameter $\nu_e \ge 0$:
$$\delta_{ij}(s) \;=\; (\theta_i - \theta_j) + s \gamma_e, \qquad P(i \succ j \mid s) \;=\; \frac{\exp(\delta_{ij}(s)/2)}{\exp(\delta_{ij}(s)/2) + \exp(-\delta_{ij}(s)/2) + \nu_e}, \qquad P(\text{draw} \mid s) \;=\; \frac{\nu_e}{Z_{ij}(s)},$$
with MAP ratings converted to standard Elo anchored at $\mathrm{Elo}(\texttt{1-pass::B0\_root@B=0}) \equiv \mathbf{1000.0}$ via $\mathrm{Elo}_i = 1000.0 + \frac{400}{\ln 10}(\theta_i - \theta_{\text{anchor}})$. To account for nontransitive rock-paper-scissors cycles in imperfect-information and strategic games (`[R1S:124]`), `fit_antisymmetric_melo_and_nash` fits the rank-2 antisymmetric decomposition $\mathrm{logit}\,P(i \succ j \mid s) = (\theta_i - \theta_j) + s\gamma_e + u_i^\top J u_j$ ($J = \bigl(\begin{smallmatrix} 0 & 1 \\ -1 & 0 \end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$) and reports both the cyclic variance share $\Omega_{\text{cyc}, e} = \frac{\|U J U^\top\|_F^2}{\|\Theta_{\text{trans}}\|_F^2 + \|U J U^\top\|_F^2}$ and the replicator-dynamics Nash equilibrium mixture $p^*_e$.

### 3.3 Rasch-2PL & Schmid-Leiman 3-Factor Bifactor `Mini-ECI` and the Mechanistic Redundancy Law (`src/eval/mini_eci.py`)

In [`src/eval/mini_eci.py`](../../src/eval/mini_eci.py) (`[ECI:1-428, PR2:92-122]`), performance across all evaluated cohorts $\tilde{a} = (A, B)$ and benchmark items $m \in \{1, \dots, M\}$ is mapped onto a unified capability scale:
1. **1D Rasch-2PL & Schmid-Leiman 3-Factor Bifactor IRT:** Each item performance $y_{\tilde{a}, m}$ is modeled by a general capability factor $\theta^{\text{gen}}_{\tilde{a}}$ plus three orthogonal domain group factors $\theta^{(\text{Ded})}, \theta^{(\text{Inf})}, \theta^{(\text{Str})}$ (`[PR2:102-110, R11:117-138]`):
   $$z_{\tilde{a}, m} \;=\; \lambda_{g, m} \theta^{\text{gen}}_{\tilde{a}} \;+\; \lambda_{s, m} \theta^{(g(m))}_{\tilde{a}} \;+\; \varepsilon_{\tilde{a}, m}, \qquad \mathrm{Mini\text{-}ECI}(A, B) \;=\; 100.0 \;+\; 15.0 \cdot \Big(\theta^{\text{gen}}_{A, B} - \theta^{\text{gen}}_{\texttt{1-pass@B=0}}\Big).$$
   Unidimensionality of the general factor is audited via the **Explained Common Variance** $\mathrm{ECV} = \frac{\sum_m \lambda_{g,m}^2}{\sum_m (\lambda_{g,m}^2 + \lambda_{s,m}^2)}$, **McDonald's hierarchical omega** $\omega_h = \frac{(\sum_m \lambda_{g,m})^2}{(\sum_m \lambda_{g,m})^2 + \sum_{G} (\sum_{m \in G} \lambda_{s,m})^2 + \sum_m (1 - h_m^2)}$, and **Leave-One-Task-Out (`LOTO`)** transfer correlation $r$.
2. **Mechanistic Redundancy Scaling Law (`[R1S:119-121, PR2:111-121]`):** Because $B$ rollouts with pairwise Gram-matrix overlap $\rho \in [0, 1)$ yield effective independent sample size $N_{\text{eff}}(B) = \frac{B}{1 + \rho(B - 1)}$, capability gain across compute budgets $B \in \{0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128\}$ follows:
   $$\Delta c_{A}(B) \;=\; \mathrm{Mini\text{-}ECI}(A, B) - \mathrm{Mini\text{-}ECI}(A, 0) \;=\; \alpha_{A} \log\!\left(1 \;+\; \frac{B}{1 + \rho_{\text{IRT}, A}(B - 1)}\right),$$
   alongside the log-linear compute slope $\kappa_{\text{search}}$ (in `Mini-ECI` points per budget doubling $\log_2(1 + B)$) and the physical `TreePagedKVBank` salience key crowding $\bar{\rho}_{\text{Gram}}(A) = \frac{1}{B(B-1)}\sum_{r \neq r'} \max(0, \langle k_r, k_{r'} \rangle)$ at $B = 32$.

## 4. Tier-1 Operator Ceiling Verification (`R10-E1..E4` & `Stage-2`, Clearly Labelled `B-SURROGATE`)

Before examining the end-to-end SGD-trained neural results on the `NVIDIA L4` GPU (`Section 5`), we report the Stage-1 (`R10-E1`–`R10-E4`) and `Stage-2` (`Mini-ECI`) verification suites under their exact Round-2 forensic audit classifications (`[R2A:18-100, R2S:60-73]`):
- **`A-EXACT` (Retained in Full):** Exact algebraic identities, autograd firewalls, spectral radius bounds, unit-module linear algebra on `TwoStageTreeClearer`, `DepthDebiasedTrustHead.pool_values_trust_shrunk`, `EpisodicMemoryTransport`, `Proposal-Poker` interventional honesty, and `BayesianEloLeague` / `MiniECIFitter` parameter recovery.
- **`A-LIVE-UNTRAINED` (Retained as Structural Wiring Proofs):** Live `TreePagedKVSearch.run_search` topology ($f_{\text{root}}, f_{\text{tip}}, f_{\text{fork}}$), `MoPSFractalPlanHead.gram_orthogonal_project` 24-sector coverage, and causal plan-swap action flip rate on initialized weights.
- **`B-SURROGATE` (Controlled Surrogate Simulation — Void as Blind Neural Evidence):** All multi-budget test KL, Brier, Witness, Elo, and `Mini-ECI` curves in `run_r10_e1_e2.py`, `run_r10_e3_e4.py`, and `run_stage2_mini_eci.py` that interpolated toward `exact_marginals` / `query_targets` or sampled from `mechanistic_redundancy_delta_c` / `btd_probabilities`.

### 4.2 Stage-2 Psychometric & League Estimator Verification (`RESULTS-STAGE2-MINI-ECI.md`, Labelled `B-SURROGATE`)

In `Stage-2` (`benchmarks/run_stage2_mini_eci.py`, runtime `483.10s`, `[ST2:1-139, JST2]`), the `BayesianEloLeague`, antisymmetric `mElo`, and 3-factor orthogonal Bifactor `MiniECIFitter` estimators were tested across `4,860` `(agent, environment, budget)` cells and `28,380` pairwise matches (`[R2A:61-70]`):
- **What `Stage-2` Validly Verified (`A-EXACT` Psychometric & Tournament Estimator Recovery):**
  1. When supplied with population trajectories generated from known redundancy parameters $(\alpha_{A,e}, \rho_{A,e})$ and `btd_probabilities` match outcomes (`[R2A:67-68]`), `fit_orthogonal_bifactor_mini_eci` and `MiniECIFitter` achieved item $R^2 \ge \mathbf{0.9677}$ (overall 1D Rasch-2PL $R^2 = \mathbf{0.9569}$), $\mathrm{ECV} = \mathbf{0.9915}$, McDonald's $\omega_H = \mathbf{0.9966}$, Leave-One-Task-Out `LOTO` $r = \mathbf{0.9657}$, and discrete-vs-continuous channel Spearman rank correlation $r_S = \mathbf{0.9828}$ across all 63 `(family, B)` cells (`STAGE2-D6`, `[ST2:7, 20]`).
  2. `BayesianEloLeague.fit_all_environments` and `fit_antisymmetric_melo_and_nash` accurately recovered latent BTD Elo ratings (`GLC-AOM-KV-Tree@B=64` mean Elo `1335.1 [1298.4, 1371.8]`, `B=128` `1386.6 [1354.9, 1418.4]`), seat biases ($\gamma_{\text{Hex}} = +30.7$ Elo, $\gamma_{\text{C4}} = +14.5$ Elo, $\gamma_{\text{Stratego}} = +10.8$ Elo), draw parameters ($\nu_{\text{C4}} = 0.158, \nu_{\text{Stratego}} = 0.222$), and cyclic variance shares ($\Omega_{\text{cyc}} \in [0.0073, 0.0129]$, `BTD` vs. Nash `mElo` Spearman $r_S = 0.9998$ overall `[ST2:70-104]`).
  3. On the Integrity Panel (`[ST2:129-138, R2A:69]`), `IP-1` ($\max|\mu_0 - q_{\text{real}}| = 0.0000$, 300-step SGD policy RL $\Delta_{\text{belief}} = 0.0000$), `IP-2` at $B=0$ ($D_{\text{bias}}(B=0) = 0.0000$), and `IP-3` (zero-shot utility-swap action flip rate `1.0000`) were genuinely executed on `CalibratedGLCAOMWorldModel`.
- **Why `Stage-2` Scaling Curves Are Reclassified as `B-SURROGATE` (`[R2A:65-69]`):**
  Because `run_stage2_mini_eci.py` generated episode KL/Brier/Witness values directly from `mechanistic_redundancy_delta_c(float(b), alpha_base * env_alpha_mult, 0.55 * rho_base + 0.45 * rho_gram)` (`lines 858, 913–922`) and smoothed `rho_gram` with per-algorithm kernel widths (`lines 608–640`), the Stage-2 `Mini-ECI` trajectories (`GLC-AOM-KV-Tree`: `99.9` [`99.86`] at $B=0 \to$ `126.2` [`126.19`] at $B=32 \to$ `129.4` [`129.38`] at $B=128$, $\Delta = +29.5$ [`+29.52`], $R^2 = 0.9982$, and $\mathrm{corr}(\hat{\rho}_{\text{IRT}}, \bar{\rho}_{\text{Gram}}) = 0.8078$ in `STAGE2-D1, D2` `[ST2:15-16, 28-64]`) represent the **analytic operator ceiling** under the postulated generative model rather than an empirical test of learned neural weights. Testing whether those scaling laws hold without circularity required executing **`R11-GPU-NEURAL`**.

## 5. End-to-End `NVIDIA L4` GPU Neural Results (`R11-GPU-NEURAL`, `A-LIVE-NEURAL`)

### 5.1 Experimental Setup, Hardware, Runtime & GCE `NVIDIA L4` Cost Ledger

Per `PREREG-R11-GPU-NEURAL.md` (`[PR11:18-36]`), all neural models in `R11-GPU-NEURAL` (`benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py`, `2,277` lines `[R3A:7]`) were initialized from scratch with random weights (`GLCAOMConfig`: $d_{\text{model}}=128, n_{\text{layers}}=4, n_{\text{heads}}=4, m_{\text{aux}}=4, d_{\text{aux}}=64, D_{\text{aux}}=256$, `2,035,868` world-model parameters, `575,475` `MoPSFractalPlanHead` parameters, and `1,030` `TwoStageTreeClearer` parameters `[R11:15, 245, JR11:model_config]`), trained end-to-end with `AdamW` on disjoint `split="train"` batches, calibrated on `split="cal"`, and evaluated on blind `split="test"` batches across **3 independent seeds (`101, 102, 103`, $t_{0.975,2} = 4.303$)**. Table 5 reports the complete GCE hardware, wall-clock runtime, and cost ledger verified against `GPU-LOG.md` (`[R11:29-44, R3A:26-36, GPULOG:2177-2189]`).

### 5.3 Detailed Results for Gate `R11-D0` (Zero Oracle Leakage: Static AST `S1`–`S6` & Runtime `PoisonTensor` `R1`–`R4`)

Before any test-time prediction in `R11-GPU-NEURAL`, `verify_zero_oracle_leakage_gate` (`benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py:420–565`) executed both static AST inspection (`src/eval/ast_leak_auditor.py` `[AST:1-302]`) and runtime canary verification across all 12 environments and all 3 seeds (`[R11:48-71, R3A:39-60]`):
1. **Static AST Audit (`S1`–`S6`, `[R11:53-60, JR11:per_seed.*.r11_d0_gate.static_ast_audit]`):**
   - `S1` (`0` violations): Zero accesses to `.exact_marginals`, `.target_tokens`, or `.query_targets` inside `src/models/glc_aom.py`, `src/search/kv_tree_search.py`, or any non-scoring function in `benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py`.
   - `S2` (`0` violations): Zero accesses to private oracle `batch.meta[...]` keys (`assignments`, `boards`, `mine_grids`, `occupancies`, `piece_grids`, `type_grids`, `signals`, `y_outcomes`, `true_type`).
   - `S3` (`0` violations): Zero dynamic reflection (`getattr`/`getitem`) with oracle key literals and zero calls to `mechanistic_redundancy_delta_c` or `btd_probabilities` inside episode/match generators.
   - `S4` (`0` violations): Zero calls to `.evaluate_beliefs` or `._solve_exact` inside model/search modules and zero seed/budget-conditional outcome branches.
   - `S5` (`0` violations): Zero oracle reads prior to `search()` completion in `run_r11_gpu_neural.py`.
   - `S6` (`3/3` synthetic leak canaries caught): `caught_S1_exact_marginals = True`, `caught_S3_law_in_generator = True`, `caught_S4_seed_budget_branch = True`.
2. **Runtime `PoisonTensor` & Canary Invariance (`R1`–`R4`, `[R11:61-71, JR11:per_seed.*.r11_d0_gate]`):**
   - **`R1` (`PoisonTensor` Sentinel Verification):** Calling `strip_oracle_fields(batch)` replaces `exact_marginals`, `target_tokens`, and `query_targets` with `PoisonTensor` sentinels that immediately raise `OracleLeakError` on any tensor operation (`3/3` traps verified on every seed).
   - **`R2` (Label-Permutation & Zeroing Canary Invariance Across All 12 Environments):** On every environment (`equiv_sat`, `sudoku_6x6`, `sudoku_9x9_extreme`, `shiphunt_6x6`, `shiphunt_8x8`, `minesweeper_6x6`, `hex_5x5`, `connect4_6x7`, `micro_stratego_4x4`, `micro_stratego_6x6`, `galanis_market_t3s111y2`, `proposal_poker`), running `model` and `TreePagedKVSearch.search` on stripped true batches vs. batches whose underlying oracle fields were randomly permuted and zeroed prior to stripping produced **`global_max_diff = 0.000000000`** on every environment in every seed (`101, 102, 103`).
   - **`R3` (SGD Parameter Movement & Loss Drop):** Across the 3 seeds, `AdamW` training shifted `GLCAOMWorldModel` parameters by $\|\theta_{\text{trained}} - \theta_{\text{init}}\|_2 = \mathbf{17.7591}$ (Seed 101), **`17.6201`** (Seed 102), and **`18.4295`** (Seed 103; 3-seed mean **`17.9362 [16.8611, 19.0114]`**, exact unrounded float mean `17.9363`), reducing training loss by **`-95.64%`** (`15.0813 -> 0.6579` mean: `10.0001 -> 0.9129` on Seed 101, `17.1429 -> 0.4818` on Seed 102, `18.1009 -> 0.5791` on Seed 103) and reducing $B=0$ blind test KL relative to the uniform prior by **`+14.85%`** (`+2.95% / +8.94% / +32.67%`).
   - **`R4` (KV-Cache Equivalence):** Incremental `TreePagedKVBank` root evaluation matched uncached sequence evaluation to **`0.000000000`** max difference in all 3 seeds.

## 6. Negative Results, Boundary Conditions, Audit Disclosures & Next Steps (`Round 12`)

### 6.1 Detailed Analysis of the Three `PARTIAL PASS` / Failing Sub-Clauses in `R11-GPU-NEURAL`

1. **Failure of the Absolute `query_kl <= 0.15` Bar in `R11-D4` (`0.8735` on `Equiv-SAT`, `1.0219` on `Sudoku-6x6` at $B=32$, `[R11:23, 196, R3A:96-99]`):**
   - *Phenomenon:* In `R11-E1`, `A4_GLC_BilinearAux_QuerySteered` improved relational query classification accuracy (`query_acc`) from `53.1%` ($B=0$) to **`58.1%`** ($B=16$) on `Equiv-SAT` and from `75.3%` ($B=0$) to **`78.5%`** ($B=16$) on `Sudoku-6x6`, while protecting first-order variable marginals (`Var KL = 0.0079` vs. `1.6535` for `A3` on `Equiv-SAT`, a `209.3x` reduction). Yet `A4`'s Bernoulli `query_kl` rose from `0.5224` ($B=0$) to `0.8704` ($B=16$) / `0.8735` ($B=32$) on `Equiv-SAT` and from `0.5157` ($B=0$) to `0.9325` ($B=16$) / `1.0219` ($B=32$) on `Sudoku-6x6`, missing the pre-registered `query_kl <= 0.15` threshold.
   - *Exact Code-Level Root Cause:* In `compute_training_step_loss` (`benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py:775–778` `[R3A:97]`), `CombinatorialQueryHead.predict_query_probs` was trained exclusively on **1-step** sibling consensus `h_cons_sib = out["h_cons"] + 0.5 * (v_exp_true + v_exp_alt)`. At test time (`_run_single_pass_all_budgets`, `line 1487` `[R3A:98]`), `h_cons_curr` accumulates cleared bilinear increments `v_exp` across up to $B=32$ tree steps without RMS depth normalization or a calibrated query temperature $\tau_q$. Consequently, the norm $\|h_{\text{cons}, Z^W_{\text{aux}}}(B)\|_2$ grows with $B$, making `query_head` logits $3\text{--}5\times$ overconfident on minority sign errors and inflating `query_kl` even as `query_acc` rises.
   - *Also Disclosed on `Equiv-SAT` `query_acc` (`A4` vs. `A3`):* On `Equiv-SAT`, ungated flat `A3_BilinearAux_RandomSearch` achieved higher `query_acc` (`69.8%` at $B=16$, `72.4%` at $B=32$ `[R11:180]`) than tree-cleared `A4` (`58.1%` at $B=16$, `57.6%` at $B=32$ `[R11:182]`) because `A3` applied unshrunk bilinear updates that simultaneously blew up `Var KL` by `209x` (`1.6535` vs. `0.0079`), whereas `A4`'s Stage-3 precision gate damped both `Var KL` and $Z^W_{\text{aux}}$ step size.

2. **Real Neural Search Gain (`+6.95` `Mini-ECI`) vs. the `+18.0` Analytic Ceiling Bar in `R11-D1` (`[R11:20, 82-84, R3A:69-75]`):**
   - *Phenomenon:* Under 100% blind test-time rollouts on SGD-trained weights, `Unified-Generalist::GLC_Tree_Active_PUCT` increased `Mini-ECI` from `98.34` ($B=0$) to `105.30` ($B=128$, **`+6.95` points**, $\kappa = +0.7637$/doubling, $R^2_{\text{log}} = 0.9241$, $R^2_{\text{red}} = 0.9155$) and `GLC-AOM-KV-Tree` increased from `99.03` to `104.46` (**`+5.43` points**, $\kappa = +0.7389$/doubling, $R^2_{\text{red}} = \mathbf{0.9443} \ge 0.92$), while lifting witness discovery from `0.52%` to **`43.06%`** (`+42.54 pp`) and mean Elo by **`+268.1`**. However, `+6.95` is below the `+18.0` `Mini-ECI` bar carried over from the Stage-2 analytic operator ceiling (`+29.5` in `ST2:16`).
   - *Root Cause:* In `R11-GPU-NEURAL`, `Unified-Generalist` was trained for only `180` total optimizer steps across 12 environments (`15` gradient steps per environment `[R3A:60, 112]`), so on high-cardinality combinatorial boards (`sudoku_9x9_extreme`: $81 \times 9$, `minesweeper_6x6`: $36 \times 2$, `micro_stratego_6x6`: $8 \times 6$), the 1-step local law is only partially learned at $B=0$ (`sudoku_9x9_extreme` $B=0$ KL is `2.8523 -> 2.5862` at $B=128$ with `0.0%` full-board witnesses `[R11:221]`). Consistent with our Round 1–3 finding (`overnight-results-paper-2026-10-15.md` Abstract `[overnight-results-paper-2026-10-15:11]`), test-time search yields large KL reductions only once the base network has learned the 1-step local law (`equiv_sat` `100.0%` witnesses, `shiphunt_8x8` `80.6%` witnesses and `-95.4%` active-plan KL cut, `shiphunt_6x6` `58.3%` witnesses).

3. **`Unified-Generalist` vs. `12x`-Parameter `Single-Game-Specialist` in `R11-D6` (`-32.6%` Lower SA KL & `4.06x` Steeper $\kappa$ vs. `-314.4` Lower Raw Multiplayer Elo at $B=32$, `[R11:25, 99-106, 147-151, R3A:109-115]`):**
   - *Phenomenon:* At $B=128$, `Unified-Generalist` (`2,035,868` shared parameters) beat the average of the 12 `Single-Game-Specialist` models (`12 x 2,035,868 = 24,430,416` total parameters) by **`-32.6%` in mean single-agent KL (`0.5319` vs. `0.7896`)**, **`-11.1%` in mean Brier (`0.2548` vs. `0.2865`)**, **`+3.65 pp` in witness rate (`43.06%` vs. `39.41%`)**, and **`4.06x` in compute scaling slope $\kappa$ (`+0.7637` vs. `+0.1881`)**. However, in multiplayer Elo at $B=32$, `Single-Game-Specialist` led `Unified-Generalist` (`1423.6` vs. `1080.3` ALL Elo; `1438.0` vs. `1123.6` mean 5-env Elo), and in `Mini-ECI` (`108.37` vs. `105.30` at $B=128$).
   - *Exact Code-Level Root Cause (`run_r11_gpu_neural.py:939–1016`, `[R3A:111-114]`):* `train_generalist_and_cohorts_for_seed` trained `Unified-Generalist` for `180` steps cycling across all 12 environments = **`15` gradient steps per environment**, whereas each `Single-Game-Specialist` was trained for `60` steps on its own family (`1` or `2` environments) = **`30` to `60` gradient steps per environment** (`2x` to `4x` more gradient updates per game!). On deductive puzzles (`equiv_sat`, `sudoku_6x6`, `sudoku_9x9_extreme`), cross-game transfer regularized the generalist (`sudoku_6x6` KL `0.9036` vs. `1.9556` for the 30-step specialist that overfit its small single-game split), whereas on strategic games (`Micro-Stratego`, `Galanis-Market`, `Proposal-Poker`), `2x–4x` more game-specific updates gave the specialists lower root KL (`0.1017` vs. `0.2717` on `Galanis-Market`; `0.1281` vs. `0.2041` on `Proposal-Poker` `[R11:229-230]`) and higher Elo.

---

### 6.2 Boundary Conditions and Forensic Audit Disclosures (`board-opus` Standard)

In accordance with Kelvin's Standing Rules and Fleet Policy 15, we explicitly disclose five boundary conditions and per-seed nuances identified during the Round-3 code and numerical audit (`[R3A:59-61, 107-108, 118-127]`):
1. **Per-Seed `R3` $B=0$ KL vs. $\theta_{\text{init}}$ on Seed 101 (`[R3A:55-60, JR11:per_seed.101.r11_d0_gate]`):** While `Unified-Generalist` reduced $B=0$ blind test KL relative to the uniform prior in all 3 seeds (`+2.95%` Seed 101, `+8.94%` Seed 102, `+32.67%` Seed 103; mean **`+14.85%`**) and reduced training loss by `90.87% / 97.19% / 96.80%`, its unweighted 12-environment $B=0$ test KL relative to random initialization $\theta_{\text{init}}$ improved on Seed 102 (`+3.60%`, `0.5244 -> 0.5055`) and Seed 103 (`+11.79%`, `0.4265 -> 0.3762`) but was `-18.94%` (`0.4654 -> 0.5535`) on Seed 101 due to high logit variance on `sudoku_9x9_extreme` after only `15` gradient steps per environment (3-seed mean vs. $\theta_{\text{init}}$: `-1.18% [-40.71%, +38.35%]`).
2. **Per-Seed `F4.4` and `F4.5` (`LIR-Masked` vs. `Fused` on Seed 101, `[R11:208-209, JR11:per_seed.101.integrity_panel]`):** On `Proposal-Poker` skewed belief KL (`F4.4`), `LIR-Masked` beat `Fused` in 3-seed mean (`0.2031` vs. `0.2612`, `-22.25%`) and strongly on Seed 102 (`0.1903` vs. `0.3252`, `-41.5%`) and Seed 103 (`0.2132` vs. `0.2582`, `-17.4%`), while Seed 101 was `0.2059` vs. `0.2004` (`+0.0055`), so the 3-seed paired Student-$t$ CI `[-0.2348, +0.1186]` crosses zero. Likewise, on `F4.5`, `LIR-Masked` beat `Fused` on 5-environment mean Elo in all 3 seeds (`+526.2` mean Elo: `1123.6` vs. `593.4`) and on `ALL`-league Elo in 3-seed mean (`1080.3` vs. `835.9`, `+244.4` ALL Elo; Seed 102 `1050.1` vs. `757.3`, Seed 103 `1120.2` vs. `670.4`), while Seed 101 `ALL`-league Elo alone was `1070.8` vs. `1080.1` (`-9.3`). Also note that on `sudoku_6x6` and `sudoku_9x9_extreme` (`[R11:220-221]`), `Fused` had lower raw `B=0` KL (`0.8565` and `1.1599`) but higher Brier (`0.3321` vs. `0.2548` `[R11:100, 111]`), lower witness discovery (`26.39%` vs. `43.06%`), and lower `Mini-ECI` (`90.15` vs. `105.30`) because `Fused` output flatter, high-entropy marginals on large-$K_{\text{dom}}$ grids that avoided high-KL spike penalties while failing to resolve cells.
3. **Self-Median Label Definition in `F5.1a` (`AUROC(m_ql) = 1.0000`, `[R3A:123-124]`):** In `benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py:1860`, `valid_leaf_lbl` was defined as `(mql_all >= float(np.median(mql_all))).astype(np.float32)`. Because threshold classification of a continuous score against its own median yields `AUROC = 1.0000` by construction, `F5.1a` in `R11-GPU-NEURAL` is a by-construction check on `m_ql` (whereas `F5.1b` `AUROC(m_qs) = 0.8038` genuinely measures the agreement between chain-time `m_qs` and leaf self-read `m_ql`). In Round 12, both `m_ql` and `m_qs` must be scored against an external ground-truth clash/witness indicator.
4. **Hybrid Interactive Board Rollouts + Blind Belief-Payoff Matches in `R11-D3` (`[R3A:121-122]`):** In `run_real_neural_multiplayer_tournaments` (`benchmarks/run_r11_gpu_neural.py:1622–1723`), `Hex-5x5` and `Connect-4` executed turn-by-turn interactive board games (`t_env.play_match(pol_a, pol_b, num_games=4)`), while the full 108-agent cross-budget matrix was connected via pairwise matches scored from each cohort's blind neural test metrics (`payoff_mean = -1.8 * kl - 0.6 * brier + 0.45 * discovery_witness_rate`). While 100% derived from blind neural forward passes (`R11-D0` verified), Round 12 separates pure turn-by-turn board match Elo from epistemic payoff Elo.
5. **Non-Decreasing vs. Strictly Monotone KL Transitions at $B = 8 \to 128$ (`[R11:99, 219-230]`):** Across the 12-environment suite, `Unified-Generalist` mean KL dropped sharply from `0.5759` ($B=0$) to **`0.5225` ($B=8$)** and remained flat-to-slightly-higher at `0.5300` ($B=32$) and `0.5319` ($B=128$, max step rise `0.0045 <= 0.015`, `6.67/8` non-increasing steps `[R11:244]`), while witness discovery continued to climb strongly from `22.92%` ($B=8$) to `27.00%` ($B=32$), `34.64%` ($B=64$), and **`43.06%` ($B=128$)** and multiplayer Elo climbed from `1087.2` ($B=8$) to **`1195.6` ($B=128$)**. This confirms `GLM-5.3`'s Dual-Channel Readout prediction (`[R1S:113]`): the Stage-3 convex gate $\alpha_b$ conservatively caps belief-channel log-odds movement once $B \ge 8$ to prevent oversearch (`Var KL` rises only slightly on `shiphunt_6x6` `0.0440 -> 0.0574` and `shiphunt_8x8` `0.0654 -> 0.0853`), while the discovery channel continues to harvest witnesses and tactical wins up to $B=128$.

---

### 6.3 Locked Round-12 Pre-Registration Protocol (`R12-GPU-NEURAL-V2`, [`benchmarks/PREREG-R12-GPU-V2.md`](../../benchmarks/PREREG-R12-GPU-V2.md))

Based on the Round-3 Society synthesis (`[R3S:95-114]`), [`benchmarks/PREREG-R12-GPU-V2.md`](../../benchmarks/PREREG-R12-GPU-V2.md) (`[PR12:1-52]`) locked four falsifiable decision gates (`R12-D0` through `R12-D3`) across Seeds `101, 102, 103` on `NVIDIA L4` GPU:
1. **`R12-D0` (Zero Oracle Leakage Gate `S1`–`S6` + `R1`–`R4`, `[PR12:28-30, R3S:113]`):** `src/eval/ast_leak_auditor.py` audits `glc_aom.py`, `kv_tree_search.py`, `run_r11_gpu_neural.py`, and `run_r12_gpu_v2.py` (`0` violations, `3/3` canaries caught), and `PoisonTensor`-stripped `sub_blind`, `sub_zeros`, and `sub_perm_blind` achieve `global_max_diff == 0.000000000` across all 12 environments $\times$ 3 seeds.
2. **`R12-D1` (Post-Depth `split="cal"` Platt/Temperature Calibration & Orthonormal Pair Basis for `CombinatorialQueryHead`, `[PR12:32-38, R3S:111]`):** Anchor `BilinearAuxLemmaEmitter` on a deterministic orthonormal pair basis ($Q_{\text{pair}}^\top Q_{\text{pair}} = I_{120}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{128 \times 120}$) with branch-clamped multi-hop naked/hidden single propagation, and fit a 2-parameter post-depth Platt/temperature scaling $(\alpha_q(B), b_q(B))$ strictly on held-out `split="cal"`, testing whether `A4_GLC_BilinearAux_QuerySteered` at $B=16$ achieves `cal_query_kl <= 0.15` nats and `query_acc >= 85%` on `Equiv-SAT` and `Sudoku-6x6` while strictly beating `A1_NoAux`, `A2_LinearAux`, and `A3_BilinearAux_RandomSearch`.
3. **`R12-D2` (Iso-Update `60 steps/env` + `HeaderFiLMAdapter` `Unified-Generalist` vs. `11.83x`-Parameter `Single-Game-Specialist`, `[PR12:40-45, R3S:112]`):** Equip `Unified-Generalist-FiLM-IsoUpdate` with zero-initialized lightweight epistemic Header-FiLM modulation (`use_header_film=True`, `2,074,290` WorldModel / `2,649,765` total shared parameters vs. `31,336,116` for the 12-env specialist ensemble — an **`11.83x` parameter reduction**) and train with matched `720` total steps (`60` steps/env).
4. **`R12-D3` (Ultra-Deep KV-Tree Search up to $B = 256$, `[PR12:47-52]`):** Evaluate blind test-time KV-tree search across $B \in \{0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256\}$ (`9` transitions) across all 12 environments on `NVIDIA L4` GPU.

### 7.2 Detailed Results for `R12-D1`: Post-Depth `split="cal"` Platt Calibration & Orthonormal Pair Basis (`R12-E1`)

Replacing unconstrained random Gaussian pair projections with the deterministic orthonormal pair basis ($Q_{\text{pair}}^\top Q_{\text{pair}} = I_{120}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{128 \times 120}$), incorporating branch-clamped multi-hop naked/hidden single propagation, and fitting a 2-parameter post-depth Platt calibrator $(\alpha_q(B), b_q(B))$ strictly on held-out `split="cal"` resolved the `R11-D4` depth-overconfidence failure on `Sudoku-6x6` and cut `Equiv-SAT` query KL to the `0.15` boundary (`[R12:47-72, JR12:r12_e1_summary_3seed]`). Table 15 reports the complete 3-seed results across all 4 arms and budgets $B \in \{0, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32\}$.

**Mechanistic Insight at $B=256$ (`Unique-Solution Deduction` vs. `Multi-Witness Partial Observability`, `[R12:146-160]`):**
1. **Strict Gershgorin Spectral Stability & Deep Tree Morphology at $B=256$:** Across all 256 expansion steps, `max_gershgorin_bound_b256` remained strictly below `1.0` in every seed (`0.999870` Seed 101, `0.999906` Seed 102, `0.999878` Seed 103 `[R12:148-152]`), while `Sudoku-9x9-Extreme` maintained balanced 3-mode tree allocation (`f_root = 0.164, f_tip = 0.750, f_fork = 0.086`, maximum tree depth `12`–`16` ply `[R12:153-154]`) and exact `0.000000` `LIR` firewall invariants (`b0_identity_max_diff = 0.0`, `grad_v_wrt_z_pi_aux_max = 0.0`, `even_parity_max_diff = 0.0`, `post_rl_belief_drift = 0.0`, `d_bias_b0 = 0.0`, `LIR` skewed belief KL `0.142` vs. `0.290` `Fused` `[R12:155-156]`).
2. **Why `Sudoku-6x6/9x9` KL Drops by `-50%` to `-56%` from $B=128 \to 256$ while `ShipHunt`/`Stratego` KL Ticks Up (`0.1294 -> 0.1387` 12-env mean):** On unique-solution combinatorial deduction tasks (`Sudoku-6x6` and `Sudoku-9x9-Extreme`), any valid witness (`check_witness == True`, `100.0%` discovered) is the unique ground-truth board, so logarithmic witness reinforcement drives epistemic KL down from `0.0743 -> 0.0330` (`-55.6%`) on `Sudoku-6x6` and `0.0726 -> 0.0366` (`-49.6%`) on `Sudoku-9x9-Extreme`. Conversely, on multi-witness partial-observability games (`ShipHunt-6x6/8x8`, `Micro-Stratego-4x4/6x6`), expanding from $B=128 \to 256$ discovers valid hidden configurations on `+5.5 pp` to `+16.6 pp` more boards (`ShipHunt-6x6`: `79.2% -> 91.7%`, `ShipHunt-8x8`: `91.7% -> 97.2%`, `Stratego-4x4`: `83.3% -> 94.4%`, `Stratego-6x6`: `55.6% -> 72.2%`), but reinforcing a single discovered witness onto the marginal distribution sharpens beliefs toward that single configuration when the exact Bayesian posterior is spread across multiple valid ship/piece placements. Conditioning witness-to-marginal reinforcement on unique-witness task headers (`GAME_FAMILY == DEDUCTION`) while routing multi-witness games exclusively through Stage-1 disjunctive Bayes marginalization directly resolves this `+0.0093` nat trade-off while preserving the **`73.61%` witness discovery rate** and **`1206.5` Elo** at $B=256$.

## 8. Rounds 13–17, Society Rounds 4–5 & 8-Monograph Literature Synthesis (`A-LIVE-NEURAL`)

Following Round 12, we executed five additional pre-registered experimental suites (`Rounds 13–17`), two adversarial multi-harness Society rounds (`Society Rounds 4–5` across `Gemini 4 Argon`, `GLM-5.3`, `GPT-6-Astra`, `Claude Opus 5.5 [1m]`, and `Claude Fable 5.1`), and an 8-monograph historical/theoretical synthesis (`docs/literature/01..08`).

### 8.1 Society Round 4 & Round 13 (`R13-ALGO-IMITATION` on `NVIDIA L4` GPU, `551.37s`, `$0.26`, `7/7` Gates `PASS`)

In `society/round4/01-expressivity-theorems-and-mcts-gap.md`, we proved **Theorem R4.1** (Depth & Topology Barrier of `C0_flat_token` and $L$-layer 1-hop parent-child message passing `C1_parent_1hop` for MCTS subtree aggregation), **Theorem R4.2** (Constant-depth $\mathsf{TC}^0$ representation of MCTS/PUCT via incremental transitive ancestor bitmasks $\mathcal{M}_{\text{desc}}$ in `C2_glc_search_v1`), and **Theorem R4.3** (the 5 classical search expressivity barriers of 1-pass `C2_glc_search_v1`: $\mathsf{NC}^1$-complete alternating `Minimax/Alpha-Beta/PNS`, non-tree `CDCL` global nogoods, `MCGS` transposition DAGs, `CFR` information-set pooling, and `Gumbel` Sequential Halving sibling percentiles, all resolved by `C3_glc_search_v2` in `src/search/neural_tree_controller.py`).

## 9. Round 15 (`R15-CURIOSITY-SUITE`: 4 Simultaneous Curiosity-Driven Experiments, `12/12` Sub-Hypotheses `PASS`)

1. **`R15-C1` (Search Topology Phase Transitions Across $4 \times 4$ $(\sigma_{\text{obs}}, \gamma_{\text{couple}})$ Grid):** Low noise + deep coupling ($\sigma=0.0, \gamma=8$) induces deep linear chains with CDCL backjumps ($d_{\max}/B = 0.6594$, $f_{\text{root}} = 0.0972$, `KL = 0.0329`), high noise + shallow coupling ($\sigma=0.60, \gamma=1$) induces wide parallel root rollouts ($f_{\text{root}} = 0.7205$, $d_{\max} = 2.67$, `KL = 0.0110`), and learned `Adaptive_GLC_v2` matches or beats the best fixed topology in **`16/16` (`100.0%`)** regime cells (`[R15, JR15:R15_C1_topology_phase_transitions]`).
2. **`R15-C2` (Zero-Shot Compositional Chimera Transfer):** On held-out hybrid games (`Micro-Stratego-4x4` and `ShipHunt-6x6`), `Chimera-3Teacher-ZeroShot` cuts epistemic KL by **`-40.52%`** (`0.1419` vs. `0.2386` best specialist on `Stratego`) and **`-41.15%`** (`0.1180` vs. `0.2005` on `ShipHunt`) zero-shot without a single gradient step on the target games, while `Chimera-RL-Finetuned` reaches `0.0888` and `0.0729` KL with `0.000000` LIR drift (`[R15, JR15:R15_C2_compositional_chimera_transfer]`).
3. **`R15-C3` ("Eureka" Persistent Lemma Crystallization):** Crystallizing high-precision auxiliary lemmas ($|\ell_{\text{aux}}| > \tau_{\text{eureka}}$) into persistent root tokens at $b \le 16$ accelerates post-crystallization KL decay slope by **`3.31x`** on `Equiv-SAT` and **`2.87x`** on `Sudoku-6x6`, cutting $B=64$ `cal_query_kl` by **`-85.05%`** (`0.0226` vs. `0.1512`) and **`-84.60%`** (`0.0191` vs. `0.1240`) with **`0.00%`** false-lemma corruption (`[R15, JR15:R15_C3_eureka_lemma_crystallization]`).
4. **`R15-C4` (The Curse of Deterministic Search in Imperfect-Information Games):** Scaling deterministic $\arg\max$ `MCTS` or `Minimax` from $B=1 \to 64$ on `Proposal-Poker`, `Galanis-Market`, and `Micro-Stratego-4x4` **increases exploitability by `+46.9%` to `+109.1%`** (collapsing mixed bluffing/hedging entropy to `0.03–0.05` nats), whereas `GLCSearch-v2` with Infoset Regret Matching monotonically reduces exploitability by **`-69.9%` to `-74.5%`** and achieves a **`79.67%`** head-to-head win rate at $B=64$ (`[R15, JR15:R15_C4_curse_of_deterministic_search]`).

## 11. Society of Thinkers Round 6: Forensic Code Audit of Wave-1 (`R18`–`R21`), `ast_leak_auditor.py v2` (`S1`–`S10`), and Runtime Counterfactual Gates (`S11`–`S14`)

### 11.1 The Five Shortcut Classes Caught in Wave-1 (`R18`–`R21`, `[R6A:25-56]`)

Following Kelvin's anti-sycophancy mandate (*"we need hard, cold results on either external benchmarks, or things that could be external benchmarks... I want numbers"* `[R6A:12]`), four Wave-1 benchmark campaigns (`R18`–`R21`) were initially executed on `NVIDIA L4` GPUs (`[R18..R21]`), passing `ast_leak_auditor.py v1` (`S1`–`S6`). Rather than accepting those `24/24 PASS` numbers at face value, Member 32 (`Gemini 4 Argon`) performed a line-by-line forensic code audit (`[R6A:16-56]`) of all four Wave-1 runner scripts (`run_r18_openspiel_stratego.py`, `run_r19_public_reasoning_benchmarks.py`, `run_r20_kyle_lmsr_futarchy.py`, `run_r21_clrs_ood_and_scaling.py`). While all core library modules (`src/envs/stratego_full.py`, `src/envs/public_reasoning_benchmarks.py`, `src/markets/kyle_lmsr_futarchy.py`, `src/models/glc_aom.py`, `src/search/neural_tree_controller.py`) were `100%` clean (`0` violations), the four Wave-1 runner scripts contained **five distinct Goodhart shortcut classes (`73` / `75` static AST `v2` violations)** that had evaded `v1` (`S1`–`S6`):
1. **Finding 1 (`R18`: `benchmarks/run_r18_openspiel_stratego.py:727–780, 1272–1475`, Rules `S7` & `S8`):** In `NeuralOpenSpielPolicy.action_probabilities`, the wrapper received a pre-computed tabular CFR reference dictionary `cfr_reference_table` (`ref_sigma`) at test time, stopped calling the PyTorch network after `48` infosets (`len(self.net_cache) < 48`), and blended `p_base` with `ref_sigma` using paradigm-specific weights `w_spec`. In `StrategoParadigmAgent.select_action`, the runner stopped calling the neural network after `16` plies (`self._st_act_calls < 16`) and multiplied a hand-coded `tactical_ev` score by hardcoded paradigm multipliers (`search_gain`, `noise_scale`, `transfer_mult`, and `0.85 * law_elo + 0.15 * emp_elo`) `[R6A:27-36]`.
2. **Finding 2 (`R19`: `benchmarks/run_r19_public_reasoning_benchmarks.py:1267, 1360–1470`, Rules `S8` & `S9`):** While all 7 neural paradigms were genuinely trained on GPU with zero `target_tokens` leakage, line `1267` (`if paradigm == "GLC_AOM_v2": best_leaf = _complete_leaf_with_observable_search(...)`) gave an external Python backtracking/A* leaf solver (`_bounded_neural_sudoku_cdcl`, `_bounded_neural_3sat_cdcl`, `solve_sokoban_astar`) with multiplier `mech_mult` **exclusively to `GLC_AOM_v2`**, while withholding that helper from `AR_GRPO`, `MDLM`, `BoN_Verifier`, `TRM_Looped`, `Searchformer`, and `Stream_of_Search` `[R6A:37-40]`.
3. **Finding 3 (`R20-E2`: `benchmarks/run_r20_kyle_lmsr_futarchy.py:656–754, 978`, Rule `S8`):** While `R20-E1` (mechanism-design shootout) and `R20-E3` (Byrnes/Neuro LIR fixes) were 100% genuine mathematical/numerical verifications, `R20-E2` (`build_real_benchmark_decentralized_dataset`) extracted only a scalar `env_sig = float(blind_b.obs_features.std().item())` from the real game batches, generated a synthetic 6-bit parity problem (`bits = torch.randint(0, 2, (n_total, 6))`), and added a warmup anchor `sym_anchor_1 = cand_tr[:, 0, 0] + cand_tr[:, 1, 0]` `[R6A:42-46]`.
4. **Finding 4 (`R21-E1`: `benchmarks/run_r21_clrs_ood_and_scaling.py:656–705`, Rule `S10`):** Inside `generate_clrs_batch`, after computing ground-truth shortest-path distance `dist_vec`, ground-truth predecessor pointers `pi_star` / `pi_oh`, and ground-truth output mask `mask_star`, lines `656–705` wrote noisy copies of those targets directly into `node_feats`, `edge_feats`, `operator_basis`, and `node_basis` (`node_feats[b, :, 8] = dist_vec + 0.04 * torch.randn(...)`, `operator_basis[b, :, :, 8] = pi_oh + ...`). Because `make_blind_poisoned()` only replaced `out_pointer` and `out_mask`, the runtime `PoisonTensor` gate reported `0.000000000` even though noisy copies of the answer sat inside `node_feats` `[R6A:47-52]`.
5. **Finding 5 (`R21-E2`: `benchmarks/run_r21_clrs_ood_and_scaling.py:1320–1353`, Rule `S8`):** After genuinely training the `0.8M`, `3.2M`, and `12.8M` `ScaledSearchModel` checkpoints and computing the $B=1$ neural `base_kl`, the inner loop over $B \in \{1, 4, 16, 64, 128\}$ evaluated analytic power-law formulas (`gain_n = 0.044 * log_n`, `gain_b = 0.165 * (1 - exp(-0.52 * log_b))`) rather than executing $B$ steps of `model.controller` `[R6A:54-56]`.

### 11.2 Upgrading `src/eval/ast_leak_auditor.py` to `v2` (`S1`–`S10`) & the 4-Model External Red-Team Audit (`S11`–`S14`, `[R6S:33-128]`)

We upgraded [`src/eval/ast_leak_auditor.py`](../../src/eval/ast_leak_auditor.py) to `v2` (`Rules S1–S10`, `7/7` synthetic canary self-tests `[R6A:59-67]`), banning test-time reference tables and call-count cutoffs (`S7`: `cfr_reference_table`, `ref_sigma`, `_st_act_calls`), paradigm-specific analytic multipliers and warmup anchors (`S8`: `search_gain`, `transfer_mult`, `w_spec`, `mech_mult`, `gain_n`, `gain_b`, `task_base_difficulty`, `sym_anchor_1`), asymmetric external solver helpers inside `if paradigm == ...` (`S9`: `_complete_leaf_with_observable_search`, `_bounded_neural_sudoku_cdcl`, `_bounded_neural_3sat_cdcl`, `solve_sokoban_astar`), and target-derived features in graph generators (`S10`: `dist_vec`, `pi_star`, `pi_oh`, `mask_star`, `aux_node_stat`). When run on Wave-1 (`run_r18`..`run_r21`), `ast_leak_auditor.py v2` catches **`73` / `75` static AST violations (`pass: False`)**, whereas across Wave-2 (`run_r22a`, `run_r22b`, `run_r23`) and all `src/` modules it confirms **`0` static AST violations (`pass: True`, `7/7` canaries caught)** `[R6S:26-29]`.

Next, we convened **Society of Thinkers Round 6** (`[R6S:6-49]`) across four independent external harnesses (`GLM-5.3`, `GPT-6-Astra`, `Claude Opus 5.5 [1m]`, and `Claude Fable 5.1`, verified with `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_MODEL_FALLBACK=1`). All four external models delivered a sharp, non-sycophantic insight: **while `S1`–`S10` catch the exact variable names used in Wave-1, any syntactic identifier blacklist is a regression test on known incidents (`B1`–`B8` bypass vectors), not a semantic security boundary** (`[R6S:35-48]`):
- **`S11` (`Order-of-Computation + Target-Redaction`), `S11-L` (`1-Hop Locality`), `S11-E` (`Permutation Equivariance`), and `S11-G` (`Infoset-Consistency & PublicView Proxy`):** Execute `gen_features(G)` strictly before `solve(G)`, verify 1-hop locality under distance-$\ge 2$ edge rewiring (`Opus 5.5`), verify node-permutation equivariance $\text{gen}(\pi G) = \pi\text{gen}(G)$, and enforce bitwise infoset invariance $\pi(h) = \pi(h')$ for all $h, h' \in I$ (`0.000000000` infoset drift in `R22A`).
- **`S12-1` (`Instance-Shuffle Call-Count Invariance`), `S12-2` (`Step-Wise Intervention Sensitivity`), and `S12-4` (`Per-Instance Ratio-Dispersion Test`):** As **`Claude Opus 5.5`** proved (`[R6-04, R6S:44]`), naive global weight perturbation `S12(ii)` fails to catch an analytic formula of a genuine $B=1$ neural output `base_kl` (`R21-E2`). Intervening on neural activations $h_t$ at step $t \ge 2$ *only* (`S12-2`) and verifying cross-instance dispersion $\mathrm{CV}_i[m_i(B)/m_i(1)] > 0$ (`S12-4`) closes that loophole completely.
- **`S13` (`Harness-Owned Helper Ledger + Symmetric Track A / Track B`) and `S14` (`Strict Autograd Detach + Paired Counterfactual Broadcast Replay`):** Enforced in `R22B` and `R23`.

## 13. Pillar 2 Symmetric Public Reasoning Shootout (`Track A` Pure-Neural vs. `Track B` Symmetric 1-Pass Propagator), Clean Zero-Leakage `CLRS-30` Extrapolation & Genuine Neural Scaling (`Wave-1 R19/R21` vs. `Wave-2 R22B` on `NVIDIA L4` GPU)

### 13.1 Execution Summary & Symmetric Public Reasoning Shootout (`R22B-E1`, `449.53s` / `449.54s`, `$0.21`, `[R22B:1-99, JR22B]`)

Executed across Seeds `101, 102, 103` on GCE `NVIDIA L4` GPU VM `soup-gpu-r22bgpu-1019030634-5697` (`449.53s` / `449.54s` wall-clock, **`$0.21`** GPU cost), [`benchmarks/run_r22b_genuine_public_reasoning_clrs.py`](../../benchmarks/run_r22b_genuine_public_reasoning_clrs.py) passed `R22B-D0` with **`0` static AST `v2` violations**, **`7/7` synthetic canaries caught**, **`0.000000000` `PoisonTensor` max diff** across all E1, E2, and E3 models, and clean `CLRS-30` input shapes (`node_feats [B, N, 6]`, `edge_feats [B, N, N, 4]`). In `R22B-E1`, all 7 paradigms (`~1.20M` parameters: `1,199,937` for `GLC_AOM_v2`, `1,199,968` for `AR_GRPO`/`MDLM`/`BoN_Verifier`/`Searchformer`/`Stream_of_Search`, `1,199,977` for `TRM_Looped`) were evaluated under **both `Track A` (`100% Pure Neural`, zero external solver helpers)** and **`Track B` (`Symmetric 1-Pass Observable Unit-Propagator` given equally to all 7 paradigms)**.

As Table 26 shows, comparing Wave-1 `R19` (`99.44% ± 0.64%` for `GLC_AOM_v2` with `_complete_leaf_with_observable_search`) against Wave-2 `R22B` (`31.11% ± 0.96%` Track A, `31.39% ± 0.48%` Track B) establishes that **`~68.1 pp`** of `R19`'s solve rate came from the external Python CDCL/A* solver helper, while **`GLC_AOM_v2` genuinely ranks #1 of 7 in both `Track A` (`+9.17 pp` over `#2 Stream_of_Search` `21.94% ± 1.73%`, $p < 0.01$) and `Track B` (`+11.39 pp` over `#2 Stream_of_Search` `20.00% ± 0.00%`, $p < 0.005$)**.

### 15.1 `R24A` Stage-3 Runtime Name-Agnostic Counterfactual Auditor (`S11`–`S14`, `12.85s` / `12.854s`, `11/11` Canaries Caught, `[R24A:1-128, JR24A, RCA]`)

In Society Round 6 (`Section 11`), all four external frontier models (`GLM-5.3`, `GPT-6-Astra`, `Claude Opus 5.5 [1m]`, and `Claude Fable 5.1`) warned that static AST blacklists (`S1`–`S10`) can be bypassed by renaming variables or embedding shortcuts inside nameless tensor indexing (`B1`–`B8`), and specified four name-agnostic runtime counterfactual invariance gates (`S11`–`S14`) needed to realize the Stage-3 methodological rigor bar (`7.85 / 10`). In **Round 24A (`R24A`)**, we implemented [`src/eval/runtime_counterfactual_auditor.py`](../../src/eval/runtime_counterfactual_auditor.py) and [`benchmarks/run_r24a_runtime_counterfactual_audit.py`](../../benchmarks/run_r24a_runtime_counterfactual_audit.py) (`12.85s` / `12.854s` elapsed runtime, `overall_pass: true`).

Table 32 reports the complete Stage-1 (`S1`–`S10` AST `v2`) + Stage-3 (`S11`–`S14` Runtime Counterfactual) audit across all Wave-2 models (`R22A, R22B, R23`), all Wave-1 runners (`R18`–`R21`), and the **`11 / 11` (`100.0%`: `7/7` AST + `4/4` Runtime `S11-Canary`..`S14-Canary`)** adversarial canary suite. Every single Wave-2 model passes all `S1`–`S14` checks (`0` violations), while every Wave-1 shortcut is caught at runtime with zero reliance on variable or function names.
